# Graph Report - devbible  (2026-09-05)

## Corpus Check
- 8691 files · ~11,550,931 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 55901 nodes · 79376 edges · 2128 communities (1370 shown, 667 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 726 edges (avg confidence: 0.83)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `a9987744`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
